        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs

## Overview

Phase 9 upgrades `artagents author test` from a passive file-diff scaffold into a live runtime replay: start the plan, auto-approve all attested steps, run code steps (dry-run or actual execution), capture `events.jsonl`, normalize it, and diff against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`. Also provides `--regenerate` for golden re-seeding.

The Phase 5 scaffold (current `_cmd_test` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`) is the foundation — we replace its `return 2` branches with actual replay logic while keeping backward compat for tests that still expect the scaffold behavior.

Key constraints:
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- Stdlib only (no new dependencies).
- Scratch directory for runs — never touch real project state.
- `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` env var gates auto-approval; NOT available in normal `start` flow.

## Phase 1: Foundation — Event Normalization Helper

### Step 1: Create `artagents/core/task/normalize.py`
**Scope:** Small — new file

1. **Create** `artagents/core/task/normalize.py` with `def normalize_event(event: dict, run_dir: str | None = None) -> dict` that:
   - Strips volatile fields: `ts`, `hash`
   - If `run_dir` is provided, replaces that path prefix in any string field value with `<RUN_DIR>`
   - Also replaces `REPO_ROOT` in string values with `<REPO_ROOT>`
   - Returns a new dict with sorted keys
2. **Design decision**: Strip `hash` entirely (don't recompute). The `ts` is already stripped in Phase 5 scaffold. Path normalization is additive. Hash-chaining depends on `ts` + `hash`, so stripping both breaks the chain — that's fine; we're comparing structure, not chain integrity.
3. **Document** the decision: hashes aren't recomputed because normalization strips fields the hash depends on. This is by design.

### Step 2: Update Phase 5 scaffold normalization to use new helper
**Scope:** Small — modify existing

1. **Update** `_strip_volatile` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py:291-308` to import and use `normalize_event` from the new module. Keep backward compatibility: the function signature doesn't change.
2. **Verify** the existing scaffold tests still pass.

## Phase 2: Core — Auto-Approval Mode

### Step 3: Implement auto-approval gate in `_dispatch_attested`
**Scope:** Small — modify `artagents/core/task/gate.py`

1. **Add** a check at the top of `_dispatch_attested` (gate.py:904): if `os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST") == "1"`, skip `validate_attested_identity` and `match_attested_command` — instead:
   - Set `attestor_kind = "author_test"`, `attestor_id = "author_test"`
   - Proceed to append `step_attested` / `item_attested` with `evidence=["source: author_test"]`
   - Continue through normal produces checks
2. **Guard**: This path MUST NOT activate unless `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`. The env var is checked at module level inside the function, not globally.

### Step 4: Update `validate_attested_identity` for author_test mode
**Scope:** Small — modify `artagents/core/task/gate.py`

1. **add** early return in `validate_attested_identity` (gate.py:1064): if `os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST") == "1"`, return `("author_test", "author_test")` immediately.
2. This keeps the auto-approval in one place and avoids duplication.

## Phase 3: Core — Runtime Replay in `_cmd_test`

### Step 5: Replace `_cmd_test` scaffold with live replay
**Scope:** Large — rewrite `_cmd_test` (artagents/orchestrate/cli.py:311-371)

1. **Replace** the existing `_cmd_test` with a new implementation that:
   - Loads the compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` (like `cmd_start` does)
   - Creates a temporary scratch project directory under `tmpfile.mkdtemp()`
   - Writes `plan.json` to the scratch project
   - Starts the run: write `active_run.json`, append `run_started` event
   - **Auto-traverse the plan**: iterate through all steps via the same gate logic as `cmd_next`, but auto-approve attested steps and either dry-run or execute code steps
   - Code steps: execute the `command` via `subprocess.run` with `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` env set to point at scratch dir. If the command is an `artagents executors run` call, it should be dry-run (or actually execute with fixture inputs)
   - After all steps complete (cursor exhausts), read the resulting `events.jsonl`
   - Normalize every event via `normalize_event(run_dir=scratch_dir)`
   - Diff against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl` (also normalized)
   - Return 0 on match, 1 on drift with unified diff
   - Clean up scratch directory

2. **Key design for code step execution**: Code steps that target `artagents executors run` need actual artifacts from the fixture. The fixture directory should provide any input files (copied into the scratch step dir before execution). For the hype/smoke fixture with minimal inputs, we'll stub the executor calls (no actual external LLM calls).

3. **Simulated execution for code steps**: Since we're in test mode and may not have the actual executors installed/configured:
   - If `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`, code steps that match `artagents executors run` can be simulated: the subprocess just writes a stub output file matching the produces path and exits 0.
   - This keeps golden tests deterministic and fast.

### Step 6: Handle the fixture inputs
**Scope:** Medium — new helper in `_cmd_test`

1. **Load fixture inputs**: Read `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture>/inputs.json` which maps produces names to stub content or file paths to copy.
2. **Copy fixture artifacts**: For each step's produces, if a fixture input file exists, copy it to the step dir before inline checks run.
3. **hoist** stub/simulation logic into a `_simulate_step_execution(step, step_dir)` helper that writes minimal valid output files.

## Phase 4: CLI — Regenerate Flag

### Step 7: Add `--regenerate` flag to `author test`
**Scope:** Small — modify argparse in `_build_parser` and `_cmd_test`

1. **Add** `test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true")` in `_build_parser()`.
2. **In `_cmd_test`**: when `--regenerate` is set, after running the plan and normalizing events:
   - Write the normalized events.jsonl directly to `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`
   - Print confirmation: "`golden regenerated: <pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl — remember to commit this if intentional`"
   - Return 0

## Phase 5: Golden Fixture — Canonical Hype Orchestrator

### Step 8: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` as a DSL pack
**Scope:** Medium — new directory + files

1. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/__init__.py` (empty)
2. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/__init__.py` (empty)
3. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/hype.py` — the canonical hype orchestration DSL:
   ```python
   from artagents.orchestrate import code, nested, orchestrator
   from artagents.verify import file_nonempty, json_file

   @orchestrator("builtin.hype")
   def hype():
       return [
           code("transcribe",
               argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.transcribe"],
               produces={"transcript": json_file("transcript.json")},
           ),
           code("cut",
               argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.cut"],
               produces={"timeline": json_file("hype.timeline.json"), "assets": json_file("hype.assets.json")},
           ),
           code("render",
               argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.render"],
               produces={"video": file_nonempty("hype.mp4")},
           ),
       ]
   ```
4. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/inputs.json` — minimal stub inputs:
   ```json
   {
     "transcribe": {"transcript": {"content": {"text": "stub transcript", "segments": []}}},
     "cut": {"timeline": {"content": {"segments": []}}, "assets": {"content": {"assets": []}}},
     "render": {"video": {"stub": "hype.mp4"}}
   }
   ```
5. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/.keep` (empty)
6. **Compile the plan**: run `artagents author compile builtin.hype` to generate `build/hype.json`
7. **Run `author test --regenerate`** to generate the initial golden `golden/smoke.events.jsonl`
8. **Add `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/build/` to root `.gitignore`**

## Phase 6: Tests

### Step 9: Update existing scaffold tests
**Scope:** Small — modify `tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`

1. **Update** `test_missing_golden_returns_2_with_phase9_message` and `test_empty_golden_returns_2_with_phase9_message`: these now become live-replay tests that fail because there's no golden. The error message should change from the scaffold "implement Phase 9" message to a new message about missing golden.
2. **sys** other tests to work with the new live-replay behavior — they should still pass.

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new test file

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_passes_against_committed_golden` — run `author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` (or equivalent qualified_id) against the committed golden, assert rc=0.
2. **Uses**: `tmp_path` based packs root or the real `artagents/packs` directory (prefer real so the golden is committed).

### Step 11: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new test file

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_reports_drift_with_unified_diff` — copy the golden to temp, modify it (e.g., change a step id), run author-test against the corrupted golden, assert rc=1 and unified diff in stdout.

### Step 12: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new test file

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_regenerate_rewrites_golden` — run with `--regenerate`, assert golden file is rewritten (size changes or content matches current run), assert rc=0.

### Step 13: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new test file

1. **Test**: `test_auto_approval_only_fires_when_env_set` — mock a plan with an attested step, run gate_command with and without `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`, assert that without it, the gate rejects (no --agent/--actor), and with it, auto-approval succeeds.

## Execution Order

1. **Step 1-2**: Create normalize.py and integrate it (foundation, cheap)
2. **Step 3-4**: Implement auto-approval in gate.py (core, independent)
3. **Step 8**: Create canonical hype pack and golden fixture (needed for integration tests)
4. **Step 5-6**: Replace `_cmd_test` with live replay (depends on 1-4, 8)
5. **Step 7**: Add `--regenerate` (depends on 5)
6. **Step 9**: Update scaffold tests (depends on 5)
7. **Step 10-13**: Run new Phase 9 tests (comprehensive validation)

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_author_cli.py` — existing compile/check/describe tests (must pass after changes)
2. `pytest tests/test_author_explain.py` — existing explain tests
3. `pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py` — updated scaffold tests
4. `pytest tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py` — focused gate unit test
5. `pytest tests/test_task_kernel_events.py tests/test_task_kernel_gate.py` — kernel tests still pass
6. `pytest tests/test_author_test_pass.py` — golden pass test
7. `pytest tests/test_author_test_drift.py` — drift detection test
8. `pytest tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py` — regenerate test
9. `pytest tests/` — full suite
10. `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` — CLI integration smoke test

        Robustness level:
        standard

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revised)

## Overview

Phase 9 upgrades `artagents author test` from a passive file-diff scaffold into a live runtime replay: start the plan, auto-approve all attested steps, execute code steps (stub outputs from fixture), capture `events.jsonl`, normalize it, and diff against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`. Also provides `--regenerate` for golden re-seeding.

The canonical DSL pack is named `builtin.hype_dsl` to avoid collision with the existing legacy `builtin/hype/` folder pack (which has `__init__.py`, shadowing a `.py` module at the same stem). The CLI supports both the existing positional `qualified_id` and new `--pack`/`--orchestrator` flags to match the issue specification.

Key constraints:
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- Stdlib only (no new dependencies).
- Scratch directory for runs — never touch real project state.
- `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` env var gates auto-approval; NOT available in normal `start`/`next` flow.

## Phase 1: Foundation — Event Normalization Helper

### Step 1: Create `artagents/core/task/normalize.py`
**Scope:** Small — new file (~80 lines)

1. **Create** `artagents/core/task/normalize.py` with:
   - `VOLATILE_FIELDS = ("ts", "hash", "run_id")` — fields stripped from every event.
   - `def normalize_event(event: dict, *, run_dir: str | None = None) -> dict`:
     * Return a shallow copy with volatile fields removed.
     * If `run_dir` is provided, scan every string value in the event dict (recursively into list items and nested dict values) and replace any occurrence of the run_dir path with `<RUN_DIR>`. Keep the relative tail: transform `/tmp/xyz/steps/step_a` into `<RUN_DIR>/steps/step_a`.
     *apattern: after stripping volatile fields and normalizing paths, return the dict with sorted keys.
   - `def normalize_events_jsonl(text: str, *, run_dir: str | None = None) -> list[str]`:
     * Parse each line, call `normalize_event`, re-serialize with `sort_keys=True`.
     * Lines that fail JSON decode are returned as-is (so malformed lines surface in diffs).
2. **Design decision**: Strip `hash` entirely (don't recompute). Reasoning: `hash` depends on `ts` (which we strip) and the previous event's hash (which is also stripped), so the chain is already broken. We're comparing event structure, not chain integrity. Document this in a module docstring.
3. **Strip `run_id`**: Every replay generates a new UUID — without stripping it, golden would never match. The normalized form of `{"kind":"run_started","run_id":"...",...}` becomes `{"kind":"run_started"}`.

### Step 2: Integrate normalize into the existing `_strip_volatile` path
**Scope:** Small — modify `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py` 

1. **Replace** the body of `_strip_volatile` (cli.py:290-308) with a call to `normalize_event` from the new module. Keep the function name and signature identical so existing callers (the scaffold diff path) work unchanged.
2. **Add** `_strip_volatile` to use `normalize_event` with `run_dir` not needed here (the Phase 5 scaffold compares already-normalized lines from the fixture, not paths).
3. **Verify** existing scaffold tests (`tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`) still pass.

## Phase 2: Core — Auto-Approval Mode (Single Entry Point)

### Step 3: Implement auto-approval short-circuit in `_dispatch_attested`
**Scope:** Small — modify `artagents/core/task/gate.py`

1. **Add** right after the docstring/imports at the top of `_dispatch_attested` (gate.py:904), but before `match_attested_command` (line 918):
   ```python
   import os
   if os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST") == "1":
       attestor_kind = "author_test"
       attestor_id = "author_test"
       args = AttestedArgs(agent=None, actor="author_test", evidence=("source:author_test",))
       # Skip match_attested_command and validate_attested_identity entirely.
       # Fall through to the existing append+decision logic below.
   else:
       # existing path
   ```
2. **This is the ONLY auto-approval site**. Do NOT modify `validate_attested_identity` separately (avoids the FLAG-CR2 overlap).
3. The `attestor_kind="author_test"` is a new value. All downstream consumers (event readers, ack tracking, audit logs) must tolerate it. Since these are all dict-based event readers (no enum switch), a new string value is accepted naturally — the only place that checks `attestor_kind` is in event-formatting helpers like `make_step_attested_event`, which accept arbitrary strings.
4. **Guard**: This path activates ONLY when `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`. Without it, `_dispatch_attested` behaves identically to Phase 5 — requiring matched command + identity validation.

## Phase 3: CLI — `--pack`, `--orchestrator`, and `--packs-root` Flags

### Step 4: Add new argparse options to author test
**Scope:** Small — modify `_build_parser` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`

1. **Add** to the `test_p` subparser (cli.py:468-470):

   - `--orchestrator`: optional, orchestrator name (e.g., `hype_dsl`)
   - `--packs-root`: optional, override packs root directory
   - `--regenerate`: store_true, regenerate golden after run
2. **Make `qualified_id` positional arg optional** when `--pack` and `--orchestrator` are both provided. Validate: if neither positional nor flags given, error. If both given, they must agree.

## Phase 4: Core — Live Replay in `_cmd_test`

### Step 5: Replace `_cmd_test` scaffold with live replay
**Scope:** Large — rewrite `_cmd_test` (cli.py:311-371)

1. **Replace** the existing `_cmd_test` with a new implementation:
   a. **Load the compiled plan**: Resolve the orchestrator and load the plan (like `_resolved_plan` does). Use `compile_to_path` if no build JSON exists yet.
   b. **Create scratch project**: `tmp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="artagents_test_")`. Construct `project_root = Path(tmp_dir) / "project"`. Write `plan.json` there (the compiled plan dump).
   c. **Start the run**: Generate `run_id = uuid.uuid4().hex`, compute `plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(project_root / "plan.json")`. Write `active_run.json` via `write_active_run("test", run_id=run_id, plan_hash=plan_hash, root=project_root.parent)`.
   d. **Append `run_started` event**: `append_event(events_path, make_run_started_event(run_id, plan_hash))`.
   e. **Set env**: `os.environ["ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"] = "1"`.
   f. **Traverse the plan**: Loop through steps using the same cursor logic. Use `derive_cursor` to reconstruct step order from events. For each step:
      - **Attested step**: Call `_dispatch_attested` with the synthetic command (the auto-approval path fires because env is set). This emits `step_attested` + inline checks.
      - **Code step**: See Step 6 (simulation).
      - **Nested step**:dispatch through the gate (ulte the gate handles auto-traversal via `_auto_traverse_to_leaf`). But since we're33 not inside `gate_command`, we need to replicate the dispatch logic. Simpler: use `gate_command` directly with the scratch project's slug and command matching `step.command`.
   g. **After loop**, read and normalize `events.jsonl`.
   h. **Diff** against golden.
   i. **Clean up** scratch dir in `try/finally`.

2. **Simplified traversal**: Instead of replicating `_auto_traverse_to_leaf`, use `gate_command` for every step. The gate automatically handles nested entries, repeat expansions, and cursor advancement. The key insight: `gate_command` already does everything we need — we just call it sequentially for each step's `command` string.

   Algorithm:
   ```python
   plan = load_plan(project_root / "plan.json")
   events_path = project_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
   slug = "test"

   # We need a slug that project_dir can find. Use a custom root.
   # The scratch project_root is <tmp>/project/
   # project_dir("test", root=<tmp>) should return <tmp>/projects/test/
   # So we need: <tmp>/projects/test/ = project_root
   # => mkdir -p <tmp>/projects/test/ and point project_root there.

   for step in plan.steps:
       if isinstance(step, CodeStep):
           decision = gate_command(slug, step.command, shlex.split(step.command), root=projects_parent)
           # After dispatch, simulate execution (Step 6)
           # Then call record_dispatch_complete(decision, 0)
       elif isinstance(step, AttestedStep):
           # Build synthetic ack command: step.command + " --actor author_test --evidence source:author_test"
           ack_cmd = step.command + " --actor author_test --evidence source:author_test"
           decision = gate_command(slug, ack_cmd, shlex.split(ack_cmd), root=projects_parent)
       elif isinstance(step, NestedStep):
           # gate_command handles nested automatically via _auto_traverse_to_leaf
           # We just call it with a no-op command; the gate will enter the nested plan,
           # iterate its steps, and exit. Actually, gate_command only dispatches ONE step.
           # For nested, we need to recursively handle children.
           # Instead: call _dispatch_code with the nested step's id and let the gate's
           # auto-traverse handle it. But nested steps don't have a command.
           # Simpler: do the same for each child of the nested plan.
   ```

   **Revised approach — bypass gate_command entirely for the replay loop**: The gate is designed for interactive CLI-driven dispatch, not programmatic replay. Instead, replicate the event emission inline:

   ```python
   import uuid, tempfile, shutil
   from pathlib import Path
   from artagents.core.task.plan import (
       AttestedStep, CodeStep, NestedStep, TaskPlan, load_plan, compute_plan_hash,
       step_dir_for_path, iter_steps_with_path,
   )
   from artagents.core.task.events import (
       append_event, make_run_started_event, make_step_dispatched_event,
       make_step_completed_event, make_step_attested_event,
       make_produces_check_passed_event, make_produces_check_failed_event,
       make_item_started_event, make_item_completed_event,
       make_for_each_expanded_event, make_iteration_started_event,
       make_iteration_failed_event, make_iteration_exhausted_event,
       make_nested_entered_event, make_nested_exited_event,
   )
   from artagents.core.task.active_run import write_active_run

   def _replay_plan(plan: TaskPlan, project_root: Path, run_dir: Path, fixture_dir: Path) -> list[dict]:
       """Execute the plan in replay mode, emitting events to events.jsonl.

       Code steps get stub outputs from fixture_dir/<step-id>/. 
       Attested steps auto-approve. Nested steps recurse.
       """
       run_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
       slug = "test"
       projects_root = run_dir  # <tmp>/  where <tmp>/projects/test/ = project_root
       plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(project_root / "plan.json")
       write_active_run(slug, run_id=run_id, plan_hash=plan_hash, root=projects_root)
       events_path = project_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"

       append_event(events_path, make_run_started_event(run_id, plan_hash))

       for path, step in iter_steps_with_path(plan):
           _replay_step(step, path, slug, run_id, events_path, project_root, projects_root, run_dir, fixture_dir)

       return read_events(events_path)

   def _replay_step(step, path, slug, run_id, events_path, project_root, projects_root, run_dir, fixture_dir):
       path_str = "/".join(path)
       repeat = getattr(step, "repeat", None)

       if isinstance(step, CodeStep):
           append_event(events_path, make_step_dispatched_event(path_str, step.command))
           _simulate_code_step(step, path, slug, run_id, project_root, run_dir, fixture_dir)
           append_event(events_path, make_step_completed_event(path_str, 0))
           if step.produces:
               _run_inline_checks_replay(step, path, slug, run_id, events_path, project_root)
       elif isinstance(step, AttestedStep):
           append_event(events_path, make_step_attested_event(
               path_str, "author_test", "author_test", ("source:author_test",)
           ))
           if step.produces:
               _run_inline_checks_replay(step, path, slug, run_id, events_path, project_root)
       elif isinstance(step, NestedStep):
           child_hash = _compute_inline_plan_hash(step.plan)
           append_event(events_path, make_nested_entered_event(path_str, child_hash))
           for child_path, child_step in iter_steps_with_path(step.plan):
               child_full_path = path + child_path
               _replay_step(child_step, child_full_path, slug, run_id, events_path, project_root, projects_root, run_dir, fixture_dir)
           append_event(events_path, make_nested_exited_event(path_str, 0))
   ```

### Step 6: Code step simulation and fixture artifact wiring
**Scope:** Medium — new helper functions in `_cmd_test` or `artagents/core/task/normalize.py`

1. **Implement `_simulate_code_step`**:
   - Look for stub outputs in `<fixture_dir>/<step-id>/`.
   - For each `produces` entry, if `<fixture_dir>/<step-id>/<produces.path>` exists, copy it to the step directory under `project_root/runs/<run_id>/steps/<step-id>/<produces.path>`.
   - If no fixture file exists, create a minimal valid stub (empty JSON for `json_file()`, empty file for `file_nonempty()` with a single newline).
   -acciThe step directory is determined by `step_dir_for_path(slug, run_id, path, root=projects_root)`.

2. **Implement `_run_inline_checks_replay`**:
   - Same logic as `_run_inline_checks` in gate.py but without requiring a `GateDecision`. Instead:
     * For each `produces` entry, find the artifact at `step_dir / entry.path`.
     * Run `entry.check.run(artifact_path)`.
     * Emit `produces_check_passed` or `produces_check_failed` accordingly.
   - Since we're in replay mode with stub outputs, checks should always pass (the stub is designed to satisfy them).

3. **Integration point**: The simulation hook lives purely in `_cmd_test` — it's not wired through gate.py. This keeps the gate unmodified (except for the auto-approval check) and makes the replay loop self-contained.

### Step 7: Normalize and diff after replay
**Scope:** Medium — within `_cmd_test`

1. **After replay**, read `events.jsonl`, normalize via `normalize_events_jsonl(run_dir=str(run_dir))`.
2. **alen** load the golden `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl` and normalize it identically (with run_dir replaced by a sentinel like `<RUN_DIR>` — since golden was generated from a different run_dir, normalize both sides with `run_dir=None` and rely on path-prefix stripping to align them). Actually: normalize both with `run_dir=None` (don't pass run_dir) and *also* strip any absolute path patterns. Better approach: after stripping `ts`, `hash`, `run_id`, scan all string values for any absolute path and replace the prefix with `<RUN_DIR>` up to but not including the `steps/` or `runs/` component. For the golden file: use `run_dir=None` and the path stripping handles both sides.
3. **Diff**: `difflib.unified_diff(norm_golden, norm_captured)`.
4. **Return**: 0 on match, 1 on drift (printing diff to stdout), 2 with clear message when golden is missing/empty.
5. **Clean up**: `shutil.rmtree(run_dir)` in a `finally` block.

## Phase 6: Golden Fixture — Canonical Hype DSL Pack

### Step 9: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype_dsl.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new file + fixture + golden

1. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype_dsl.py`:
   ```python
   """Canonical V1 DSL orchestration: builtin.hype_dsl."""
   from artagents.orchestrate import code, orchestrator
   from artagents.verify import file_nonempty, json_file

   @orchestrator("builtin.hype_dsl")
   def hype_dsl():
       return [
           code("transcribe",
               argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.transcribe"],
               produces={"transcript": json_file("transcript.json")},
           ),
           code("cut",
               argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.cut"],
               produces={"timeline": json_file("hype.timeline.json"), "assets": json_file("hype.assets.json")},
           ),
           code("render",
               argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.render"],
               produces={"video": file_nonempty("hype.mp4")},
           ),
       ]
   ```
2. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/fixtures/smoke/`:
   - `inputs.json`: minimal stub inputs mapping step_id -> produces_name -> stub content
   - `transcribe/transcript.json`: `{"text": "stub transcript", "segments": []}`
   - `cut/hype.timeline.json`: `{"segments": []}`
   - `cut/hype.assets.json`: `{"assets": []}`
   - `render/hype.mp4`: empty file (just needs to exist for `file_nonempty`)
3. **Compile**: Run `artagents author compile builtin.hype_dsl` to generate `build/hype_dsl.json`.
4. **Create initial golden**: Run `artagents author test builtin.hype_dsl --fixture smoke --regenerate` to seed `golden/smoke.events.jsonl`.
5. **The `build/` directory is already gitignored** (`.gitignore` line 60: `artagents/packs/*/build/`). The golden file and fixture are committed.

## Execution Order

1. **Step 1-2**: Create normalize.py and integrate it (foundation, cheap).
2. **Step 3**: Implement auto-approval in gate.py (core, independent).
3. **Step 4**: Add new CLI flags (unblocks testability).
4. **Step 9-10**: Create canonical hype_dsl pack and fixture (needed for integration tests).
5. **Step 5-7**: Replace `_cmd_test` with live replay (depends on 1-4, 9).
6. **Step 8**: Add `--regenerate` (depends on 5).
7. **Step 11**: Update scaffold tests (depends on 5).
8. **Step 12-15**: Run new Phase 9 tests (comprehensive validation).

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_author_cli.py` — existing compile/check/describe tests (must pass after changes).
2. `pytest tests/test_author_explain.py` — existing explain tests.
3. `pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py` — updated scaffold tests.
4. `pytest tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py` — focused gate unit test.
5. `pytest tests/test_task_kernel_events.py tests/test_task_kernel_gate.py` — kernel tests still pass.
6. `artagents author compile builtin.hype_dsl` — generate build artifact.
7. `artagents author test builtin.hype_dsl --fixture smoke --regenerate` — seed golden.
8. `pytest tests/test_author_test_pass.py` — golden pass test.
9. `pytest tests/test_author_test_drift.py` — drift detection test.
10. `pytest tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py` — regenerate test.
11. `pytest tests/` — full suite.
12. `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype_dsl --fixture smoke` — CLI integration smoke test.

    "idea": "Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).\n\nLAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.\n\nDRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.\n\n\nPHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):\n\n- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.\n- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).\n- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.\n\nEXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):\n- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.\n\nWHAT TO IMPLEMENT:\n\n1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):\n   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`\n   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.\n   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` \u2014 fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.\n   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).\n   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths \u2014 keep only structural fields).\n   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.\n   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.\n\n2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):\n   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.\n   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow \u2014 only `author test` sets the env var.\n\n3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):\n   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).\n   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.\n   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) \u2014 recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.\n\n4. Regenerate workflow:\n   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.\n   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.\n\n5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` \u2014 minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` \u2014 the expected event sequence.\n\n6. Tests:\n   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.\n\nCONSTRAINTS:\n- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.\n- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.\n- No new dependencies (stdlib only).\n- The author-test must NOT touch real project state \u2014 runs in scratch dirs.\n- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.\n\nSTOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.",
    "significant_flags": 18,
    "unresolved_flags": [

        "id": "FLAG-CR1",
        "concern": "Canonical hype DSL pack collides with existing folder pack: resolver requires builtin/hype.py but plan creates builtin/hype/hype.py, and builtin/hype/ already exists as a directory. Qualified id 'builtin.hype' cannot resolve to the new DSL without renaming or removing the legacy pack.",
        "resolution": "Addressed all 17 significant flags by: (1) renaming the canonicaul DSL pack to `builtin.hype_dsl` to avoid collision with the legacy `builtin/hype/` folder pack (FLAG-CR1/correctness-1/scope-1); (2) collapsing auto-approval into a single entry point at the top of `_dispatch_attested` that short-circuits both `match_attested_command` and `validate_attested_identity` when `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` (FLAG-CR2/correctness-2/callers); (3) adding `run_id` to the normalized volatile field set (FLAG-CR3/correctness-3); (4) naming the concrete simulation dispatch site as the `_cmd_test` replay loop itself, with helper functions `_simulate_code_step` and `_run_inline_checks_replay` \u2014 not wired through gate.py (FLAG-CR4/correctness-4); (5) adding `--pack`, `--orchestrator`, and `--packs-root` CLI flags alongside the existing positional `qualified_id` (issue_hints-1/all_locations-1); (6) implementing `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>` path substitution per the issue spec (issue_hints-2); (7) storing `source:author_test` inside the evidence tuple, which flows into the existing `step_attested` event structure (issue_hints-3); (8) updating `_cmd_new` to scaffold `inputs.json` (all_locations-2); (9) noting that `build/` is already gitignored by line 60 of `.gitignore` (all_locations-3); (10) updating scaffold tests to reflect the new live-replay behavior with Phase 9 messaging (scope-2)."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-CR3",
        "concern": "Normalization omits run_id. Without deterministic run_id substitution, every replay drifts and the golden never matches.",
        "resolution": "Addressed all 17 significant flags by: (1) renaming the canonicaul DSL pack to `builtin.hype_dsl` to avoid collision with the legacy `builtin/hype/` folder pack (FLAG-CR1/correctness-1/scope-1); (2) collapsing auto-approval into a single entry point at the top of `_dispatch_attested` that short-circuits both `match_attested_command` and `validate_attested_identity` when `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` (FLAG-CR2/correctness-2/callers); (3) adding `run_id` to the normalized volatile field set (FLAG-CR3/correctness-3); (4) naming the concrete simulation dispatch site as the `_cmd_test` replay loop itself, with helper functions `_simulate_code_step` and `_run_inline_checks_replay` \u2014 not wired through gate.py (FLAG-CR4/correctness-4); (5) adding `--pack`, `--orchestrator`, and `--packs-root` CLI flags alongside the existing positional `qualified_id` (issue_hints-1/all_locations-1); (6) implementing `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>` path substitution per the issue spec (issue_hints-2); (7) storing `source:author_test` inside the evidence tuple, which flows into the existing `step_attested` event structure (issue_hints-3); (8) updating `_cmd_new` to scaffold `inputs.json` (all_locations-2); (9) noting that `build/` is already gitignored by line 60 of `.gitignore` (all_locations-3); (10) updating scaffold tests to reflect the new live-replay behavior with Phase 9 messaging (scope-2)."
      }

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (v3)

## Overview

Phase 9 upgrades `artagents author test` from a passive file-diff scaffold into a live runtime replay that exercises the real `gate_command` dispatch path: start the plan, loop through `gate_command` to advance the cursor, auto-approve attested steps (gated by `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`), simulate code-step outputs from fixture stubs, capture `events.jsonl`, normalize it, and diff against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`. Also provides `--regenerate` for golden re-seeding.

**Key correction from v2**: The canonical DSL pack uses `builtin.hype` (not `builtin.hype_dsl`). Audit confirmed there is NO pre-existing `builtin/hype/` folder — the `hype` name is available. This matches the stop condition literally.

**Key design correction**: The replay loop NOW routes through `gate_command` for every step (resolving correctness-2, correctness-3, FLAG-CR7, and scope-1 in one move). The only gate change is the `_dispatch_attested` auto-approval short-circuit. Code-step simulation happens *after* `gate_command` returns by writing stub files and calling `record_dispatch_complete`.

Key constraints:
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- Stdlib only (no new dependencies).
- Scratch directory for runs — never touch real project state.
- `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` env var gates auto-approval; NOT available in normal `start`/`next` flow.

## Phase 1: Foundation — Event Normalization Helper

### Step 1: Create `artagents/core/task/normalize.py`
**Scope:** Small — new file (~90 lines)

1. **Create** `artagents/core/task/normalize.py` with:
   - `VOLATILE_FIELDS = ("ts", "hash", "run_id")` — fields stripped from every event.
   - `def normalize_event(event: dict, *, run_dir: str | None = None) -> dict`:
     * Return a shallow copy with volatile fields removed.
     * Scan every string value in the event dict (recursively into list items and nested dict values). Replace any absolute-path prefix that looks like a run directory (contains `/runs/` or `/steps/` in the path) with `<RUN_DIR>/`. Keep the relative tail: transform `/tmp/xyz/steps/step_a` into `<RUN_DIR>/steps/step_a`.
     * If `run_dir` is provided, also scan for that specific prefix and replace it with `<RUN_DIR>`.
     * Return the dict with sorted keys.
   - `def normalize_events_jsonl(text: str, *, run_dir: str | None = None) -> list[str]`:
     * Parse each line, call `normalize_event`, re-serialize with `sort_keys=True`.
     * Lines that fail JSON decode are returned as-is (so malformed lines surface in diffs).
2. **Design decision**: Strip `hash`, `ts`, and `run_id` entirely (don't recompute). Reasoning: `hash` depends on `ts` (which we strip) and the previous event's hash (which is also stripped), so the chain is already broken. `run_id` is regenerated per replay. We're comparing event structure, not chain integrity. Document this in the module docstring.
3. **Path normalization**: Always active — scan all string values for absolute path patterns. Use a simple heuristic: if a string value starts with `/` and contains `runs/` or `steps/`, replace the prefix up to (but not including) the first occurrence of `runs/` or `steps/` with `<RUN_DIR>/`. This handles both the captured events and the golden file without needing the exact run_dir.

### Step 2: Integrate normalize into the existing `_strip_volatile` path
**Scope:** Small — modify `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`

1. **Replace** the body of `_strip_volatile` (cli.py:290-308) with a call to `normalize_event` from the new module. Keep the function name and signature identical so existing callers work unchanged.
2. **Add `run_id`** to `_VOLATILE_EVENT_FIELDS` (cli.py:287): change from `("ts", "hash")` to `("ts", "hash", "run_id")`.
3. **Update `normalize_event` call**: pass `run_dir=None` since the existing scaffold tests compare fixture-captured events (no live run_dir). Path stripping in normalize_event handles both sides.
4. **Verify** existing scaffold tests (`tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`) still pass.

## Phase 2: Core — Auto-Approval Mode in Gate

### Step 3: Implement auto-approval short-circuit in `_dispatch_attested`
**Scope:** Small — modify `artagents/core/task/gate.py`

1. **Add** right after the function signature/docstring at the top of `_dispatch_attested` (gate.py:904), before `match_attested_command` (line 918):
   ```python
   import os
   if os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST") == "1":
       attestor_kind = "author_test"
       attestor_id = "author_test"
       args = AttestedArgs(agent=None, actor="author_test", evidence=("source: author_test",))
       # Skip match_attested_command and validate_attested_identity entirely.
       # Fall through to the existing append+decision logic below.
   else:
       # existing path
   ```
2. **This is the ONLY auto-approval site**. The short-circuit jumps over `match_attested_command` and `validate_attested_identity`, directly to the `append_event` + `GateDecision` construction.
3. The `attestor_kind="author_test"` is a new value. All downstream consumers (event readers, ack tracking, audit logs) must tolerate it. Since these are all dict-based event readers (no enum switch), a new string value is accepted naturally. The evidence tuple contains `"source: author_test"` (with space, matching the issue spec).
4. **Guard**: This path activates ONLY when `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`. Without it, `_dispatch_attested` behaves identically to Phase 5.

## Phase 3: CLI — `--pack`, `--orchestrator`, `--packs-root`, `--regenerate` Flags

### Step 4: Add new argparse options to author test
**Scope:** Small — modify `_build_parser` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`

1. **Make `qualified_id` positional arg optional** for the `test` subparser: `nargs="?"`.
2. **Add** to the `test_p` subparser (after line 470):
   - `--pack`: optional, pack name (e.g., `builtin`)
   - `--orchestrator`: optional, orchestrator name (e.g., `hype`)
   - `--packs-root`: optional, override packs root directory (Path)
   - `--regenerate`: store_true, regenerate golden after run
3. **Validate** in `main()`: if neither `qualified_id` nor both `--pack`/`--orchestrator` are given, error. If both given, `qualified_id` takes precedence.
4. **Update** `main()` (cli.py:482-492) to construct `qid` from `--pack`/`--orchestrator` if `qualified_id` not given. Pass `packs_root` from `--packs-root` if provided (overrides the `main(packs_root=...)` kwarg). Pass `regenerate` flag to `_cmd_test`.

## Phase 4: Core — Live Replay in `_cmd_test` via `gate_command`

### Step 5: Replace `_cmd_test` scaffold with live replay through `gate_command`
**Scope:** Large — rewrite `_cmd_test` (cli.py:311-371)

1. **New algorithm** — route EVERY step through `gate_command` to exercise the real gate path:
   a. **Load the compiled plan**: Resolve the orchestrator and compile to a build JSON. Load the plan and fixture stubs.
   b. **Create scratch project**: `tmp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="artagents_test_")`. Construct `projects_root = Path(tmp_dir) / "projects"`. Create `project_root = projects_root / "test"`. Write `plan.json` there (the compiled plan dump).
   c. **Start the run**: Generate `run_id = uuid.uuid4().hex`, compute `plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(project_root / "plan.json")`. Write `active_run.json` via `write_active_run("test", run_id=run_id, plan_hash=plan_hash, root=Path(tmp_dir))`.
   d. **Set env**: `os.environ["ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"] = "1"` (restore in finally).
   e. **Loop**: while True:
      - Call `gate_command("test", step.command, argv_tokens, root=Path(tmp_dir))`.
        * For the first call (run_started not yet emitted), we need to emit `run_started` first and seed the events file. **Better approach**: emit `run_started` via `append_event` BEFORE entering the loop, then start the loop.
        * For each subsequent call, `gate_command` auto-traverses to the next leaf step.
      - If `gate_command` raises `TaskRunGateError` with "plan is exhausted", break.
      - The returned `GateDecision` tells us the step kind.
      - **For CodeStep**: the `step_dispatched` event was already emitted by `_dispatch_code`. Now simulate execution: write stub outputs to the step directory. Then call `record_dispatch_complete(decision, 0)` to emit `step_completed` and run inline checks.
      - **For AttestedStep**: the auto-approval fired (env var set), so `step_attested` was already emitted. `record_dispatch_complete` is a no-op for attested steps (gate.py:1099-1101). No simulation needed — attested steps don't produce artifacts.
      - Continue loop.
   f. **After loop**, read `events.jsonl` from `project_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"`.
   g. **Normalize** both captured events and golden file via `normalize_events_jsonl`.
   h. **Diff**: `difflib.unified_diff(norm_golden, norm_captured)`.
   i. **Clean up** scratch dir in `try/finally`.

2. **The `argv_tokens`** for `gate_command`: for code steps, use the step's `argv` list (from the compiled plan). For attested steps, we can use `shlex.split(step.command + " --agent author_test --evidence source: author_test")`. But since the auto-approval short-circuits before `match_attested_command`, any argv works — we just need the command string to be non-empty. Use `["ack", step.id]` as a minimal argv.

3. **run_started emission**: Before the loop, we need to seed the events file with `run_started`. Use `append_event(events_path, make_run_started_event(run_id, plan_hash))`. The `append_event` call handles hash-chaining from ZERO_HASH.

4. **Code step simulation**: After `gate_command` returns a CodeStep decision:
   - Determine `step_dir = step_dir_for_path("test", run_id, decision.plan_step_path, root=Path(tmp_dir))`.
   - For each `produces` entry in `decision.produces`, look for a stub at `<fixture_dir>/<step_id>/<produces_name>`.
   - If found, copy it to `step_dir / produces_entry.path`.
   - If not found, create a minimal valid stub: empty JSON `{}` for `json_file()`, a single newline for `file_nonempty()`.
   - Then call `record_dispatch_complete(decision, 0)` — this emits `step_completed` and runs `_run_inline_checks` against the stubs.

5. **Integration point**: The simulation hook lives in `_cmd_test` (or a helper `_simulate_code_step`). It's not wired through gate.py — it's the post-dispatch artifact seeding.

6. **Important**: `gate_command` validates `active_run.json`, chain integrity, and plan_hash. All these are set up in the scratch dir. The events file starts with just `run_started` and grows with each `gate_command` call.

### Step 6: Implement `_simulate_code_step` helper in `_cmd_test`
**Scope:** Medium — new function in cli.py or normalize.py

1. **Implement `_simulate_code_step(decision, fixture_dir, run_dir)`**:
   - For each `produces` entry in `decision.produces`:
     * Compute `step_dir` from the decision.
     * Look for `<fixture_dir>/<last-path-segment>/<produces_entry.path>`.
     * If found, copy to `step_dir / produces_entry.path`.
     * If not found, synthesize: for `json_file()` produces, write `{}`. For `file_nonempty()`, write `"\n"`.
2. **Implement `_run_inline_checks_replay`**: Not needed — `record_dispatch_complete` already calls `_run_inline_checks` from gate.py. We just need the stubs to be in place before calling it.

### Step 7: Normalize and diff after replay
**Scope:** Medium — within `_cmd_test`

1. **After replay**, read `events.jsonl`, normalize via `normalize_events_jsonl(run_dir=str(run_dir))`.
2. **Load** the golden `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl` and normalize identically (with `run_dir=None` — path-stripping in normalize_event handles both sides via the heuristic).
3. **Diff**: `difflib.unified_diff(norm_golden, norm_captured)`.
4. **Return**: 0 on match, 1 on drift (printing diff to stdout), 2 with clear message when golden is missing/empty.
5. **Clean up**: `shutil.rmtree(run_dir)` in a `finally` block.

## Phase 5: Regenerate Flag

### Step 8: Implement `--regenerate` in `_cmd_test`
**Scope:** Small — modify `_cmd_test`

1. **After normalization**, if `--regenerate` is set:
   - Write the normalized lines (joined with newlines) to `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
   - Print: `golden regenerated: <pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl — remember to commit this if intentional`.
   - Return 0.

## Phase 6: Golden Fixture — Canonical Hype DSL Pack

### Step 9: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new file + fixture + golden

1. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py`:
   ```python
   """Canonical V1 DSL orchestration: builtin.hype."""
   from artagents.orchestrate import code, orchestrator
   from artagents.verify import file_nonempty, json_file

   @orchestrator("builtin.hype")
   def hype():
       return [
           code("transcribe",
               argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.transcribe"],
               produces={"transcript": json_file("transcript.json")},
           ),
           code("cut",
               argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.cut"],
               produces={"timeline": json_file("hype.timeline.json"), "assets": json_file("hype.assets.json")},
           ),
           code("render",
               argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.render"],
               produces={"video": file_nonempty("hype.mp4")},
           ),
       ]
   ```
2. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/fixtures/smoke/`:
   - `transcribe/transcript.json`: `{"text": "stub transcript", "segments": []}`
   - `cut/hype.timeline.json`: `{"segments": []}`
   - `cut/hype.assets.json`: `{"assets": []}`
   - `render/hype.mp4`: single newline (satisfies `file_nonempty`)
3. **Compile**: Run `artagents author compile builtin.hype` to generate `build/hype.json`.
4. **Create initial golden**: Run `artagents author test builtin.hype --fixture smoke --regenerate` to seed `golden/smoke.events.jsonl`.
5. **The `build/` directory is already gitignored** (`.gitignore` line 60: `artagents/packs/*/build/`). The golden file and fixtures are committed.

### Step 10: Update `_cmd_new` to scaffold `inputs.json` in fixture dir
**Scope:** Small — modify `_cmd_new` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`

1. **Update** the fixture scaffold (cli.py:265-276): after creating `fixtures_dir` and `.keep`, also create `fixtures_dir / "inputs.json"` with an empty object `{}` (no `_comment` key — just `{}`). Add a template comment above the write in the code, not in the JSON.

## Phase 7: Tests

### Step 11: Update existing scaffold tests
**Scope:** Small — modify `tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`

1. **Update** `test_missing_golden_returns_2_with_phase9_message` and `test_empty_golden_returns_2_with_phase9_message`: The live-replay now runs the plan, but the golden is missing/empty. The return code should now be 2 with a message saying `"no golden file found at ...; run with --regenerate to create it"` instead of the old Phase 9 scaffolding message.
2. **Update** `test_matching_golden_and_captured_returns_0` and `test_mismatched_golden_returns_1_with_unified_diff`: These continue to test the normalized diff path. The underlying `_strip_volatile` now uses `normalize_event` — behavior is equivalent (same fields stripped, plus `run_id`). They should continue to pass.

### Step 12: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new test file (~50 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_passes_against_committed_golden`:
   - Run `author_cli.main(["test", "--pack", "builtin", "--orchestrator", "hype", "--fixture", "smoke"])`.
   - Assert rc=0.
2. **Test**: `test_author_test_passes_with_positional_qid`:
   - Run `author_cli.main(["test", "builtin.hype", "--fixture", "smoke"])`.
   - Assert rc=0 (backward compat).

### Step 13: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new test file (~60 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_reports_drift_with_unified_diff`:
   - Create a temp packs root, copy the compiled plan, fixtures, and committed golden.
   - Corrupt one line in the golden (e.g., change `"transcribe"` to `"transcribe_corrupted"`).
   - Run author-test against the corrupted golden with `--packs-root` pointing at the temp dir.
   - Assert rc=1, stdout contains unified diff with `---` / `+++` and the corruption visible.

### Step 14: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new test file (~50 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_regenerate_rewrites_golden`:
   - Use temp packs root with a simple plan, fixtures, and an empty golden.
   - Run with `--regenerate`.
   - Assert golden file now exists with non-empty content.
   - Assert rc=0.

### Step 15: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Medium — new test file (~80 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_auto_approval_fires_with_env_set`:
   - Set up a minimal plan with an AttestedStep in a scratch project.
   - Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`.
   - Call `gate_command` with a synthetic ack command.
   - Assert `decision.step_kind == "attested"` and events contain `step_attested` with `attestor_kind="author_test"` and `"source: author_test"` in evidence.
2. **Test**: `test_auto_approval_rejected_without_env`:
   - Same setup, but WITHOUT `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST`.
   - Call `gate_command` with an incomplete ack command (missing `--agent`/`--actor`).
   - Assert `TaskRunGateError` is raised (gate rejects).
3. **Test**: `test_auto_approval_not_accessible_from_start`:
   - Verify that the normal `start` flow does NOT set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST`. This is verified by test 15.2 (the gate rejects without the env var).

## Execution Order

1. **Step 1-2**: Create normalize.py and integrate it (foundation, cheap).
2. **Step 3**: Implement auto-approval in gate.py (core, independent).
3. **Step 4**: Add new CLI flags (unblocks testability).
4. **Step 9-10**: Create canonical hype DSL pack and fixture (needed for integration tests).
5. **Step 5-7**: Replace `_cmd_test` with live replay through `gate_command` (depends on 1-4, 9).
6. **Step 8**: Add `--regenerate` (depends on 5).
7. **Step 11**: Update scaffold tests (depends on 5).
8. **Step 12-15**: Run new Phase 9 tests (comprehensive validation).

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_author_cli.py` — existing compile/check/describe tests (must pass after changes).
2. `pytest tests/test_author_explain.py` — existing explain tests.
3. `pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py` — updated scaffold tests.
4. `pytest tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py` — focused gate unit test.
5. `pytest tests/test_task_kernel_events.py tests/test_task_kernel_gate.py` — kernel tests still pass.
6. `artagents author compile builtin.hype` — generate build artifact.
7. `artagents author test builtin.hype --fixture smoke --regenerate` — seed golden.
8. `pytest tests/test_author_test_pass.py` — golden pass test.
9. `pytest tests/test_author_test_drift.py` — drift detection test.
10. `pytest tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py` — regenerate test.
11. `pytest tests/` — full suite.
12. `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` — CLI integration smoke test.
